## R31.12 radio FLICKER-NO-STICK root+fix 2026-07-27 (commit 95ef91c36) — v0.7.145 device progress
Tron v0.7.145: tap FIRES now (my id/for+block+belt worked) but flickers-not-changing = check-then-REVERT. ROOT = MULTI-FIRE per tap (the double-handler I flagged in the spec): (a) belt binds BOTH click AND pointerup -> set() 2x; (b) label REDUNDANT (wraps input AND for= to it) -> browser dispatches radio click 2x (wrap+for= double = flicker-no-commit). RULED OUT: re-render (only ROOM_CONFIG_UPDATED->render, fires post-SAVE not on tap; radio tap sends nothing to server) + name-group (intact). ## ★ R31.7 config-flush regression — DIAGNOSED + durable GUARD designed 2026-08-05 (commit 50e7285da)
CONFIRMED on disk: config-singleton-0000 dirty M, VERSION-ONLY diff 0.8.54->0.8.46 (name/branch/plantumlUrl unchanged) = running server re-serialized the BUILD-OWNED config unit at its stale boot version; R31.7 build then regenerates pkg/sw/manifest FROM the dirty unit -> regression. Antipattern: served-repo real-saves a build-owned unit.
WRITE CHOKEPOINT = ScenarioIndex.put (src/ts/scenario/index-store.ts:43, fs.writeFileSync — SOLE disk-write for ALL units). config literal is READ-only in server.ts (:1286 plantumlUrl; /api/config=BOOT_VERSION not the unit) -> flush is a VARIABLE-uuid idx.put -> guard the chokepoint blocks ALL paths.
GUARD (by-construction, handed expert): static BUILD_OWNED_UUIDS=Set(['config-singleton-0000-000000000001']) + top-of-put() 'if BUILD_OWNED.has(uuid) { console.warn(...); return; }'. Server can NEVER persist config (read-only at runtime); build (start.mjs) owns/writes it; server reads config+serves BOOT_VERSION. ONE chokepoint=every path blocked, cannot recur; WARN surfaces offenders.
Expert folding guard into his 0.8.55 immediate fix on remoteShells:0.2 NOW (I'm OFF it per his ask). On his ship: I backstop (put-config=warn+no-op, config-singleton stays git-clean across server operation, /api/config==0.8.55). Multi-owner guard STILL parked. S36 R36.1/2 part-2 detailed reconcile design (0f13d9d87) still pending expert build after this.

(1) MODEL_STORE-isolated (prod untouched, same as siblings); (2) idempotent (keyToUuid hash, no dup/overwrite-identical); (3) NO path-traversal (inputs=uuids+relation -> hashed shard, not caller paths); (4) NO sacred-gate/prod/credential exposure (the owner-gate protects /server-manager+prod+feature-grants; MDA demo store is separate, resettable, isolated); (5) trace-create IS part of the same diagram-editing drag-loop as the ungated add-view/move-view -> uniform posture. The lone requireFeatureAccessHttp only restricts WHICH feature-members can author traces in the DEMO store -> removing it matches the ungated siblings. ALT (gate-ALL-MDA-editing) is coherent-but-heavier; ASK direction = un-gate -> RECOMMEND un-gate for drag-loop consistency. NO prod risk either way.
NEXT: report T36.4 verdict to PO; re-derive R36.1 diagnosis + T36.5 display-design specifics. Multi-owner guard STILL parked.

## ★ IDENTITY-FAMILY DETECTOR += REGISTERED-SENTINEL exclusion (PO inverse-problem) 2026-08-08 (commit 16058d17f)
The detector kills fabricated identities but must NOT flag DELIBERATE patterned ones (metamodel sentinels). S40 UmlNode a1d2e3f4-0000-4a1b-8c2d-000000000021 joins the M2 catalogue family (a1d2e3f4-...0003=UmlClass) — patterned ON PURPOSE (random v4 breaks M2-family lookup) but my entropy rule flags it. RULE: fabricated = patterned AND NOT isRegisteredSentinel. isRegisteredSentinel reads a REGISTRY the detector consults (PROVABLE not a hand-maintained skip-list): (a) M2 metamodel catalogue (a1d2e3f4-... family, by FAMILY not per-uuid) + (b) system constants (00000000 root, CurrentSprint). Mint-path REGISTERS deliberate sentinels; mint-or-refuse still rejects unregistered patterned. Both-directions rule: tool removing fiction must not erase deliberate truth. RECORDED, build later (S40 first). Detector-build must include registered-sentinel from the start.

## ★ R40.14 web4ID LOGINTOKEN DESIGN 2026-08-08 (Tron GO, credential, commit bcc33ded3, design-r40.14-web4id-logintoken.md) — DESIGN-ONLY, HOLD
req e8ab7aa2 (R40.14) Backlog/S40. Security design (portable encrypted loginToken credential) — detail withheld from this PUBLIC repo per the security-doc process. ## ★★ ROTATION-SCOPE RULING 2026-08-09 (measured): DECOUPLE credential from identity, do NOT rename. The owner literal is BOTH the identity PRIMARY KEY (getUserHomeDir->data/users/<token>/, ownerToken 20 refs, profile-unit uuid==token, allowedUsers=token values) AND the bearer credential (assertOwner timingSafeEqual, tokenToClient, x-player-token) = the same value = root flaw.
